%MACRO CONVERT_REF_LTV (MES_AUX, MES_SAIDA);

DATA AUX;
LENGTH	AUX $6.
		MES_LTV $7.;
AUX = &MES_AUX;
MES_LTV = CAT(SUBSTR(AUX,1,4),'_',SUBSTR(AUX,5,2));

DROP AUX;

RUN;

PROC SQL NOPRINT;
SELECT DISTINCT(MES_LTV) INTO :&MES_SAIDA
FROM AUX;

%LET &MES_SAIDA = &&&MES_SAIDA;

QUIT;

PROC DELETE DATA= AUX;



%MEND CONVERT_REF_LTV;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: 00. Start SASApp */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 001 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: 00. Macro Clusters */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 002 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 84 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: 00. Macro Clusters */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-xf7LGLBAmU2rre1M/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%MACRO APLICA_CLUSTER (FL_BASE, PRODUTO, QTATRASO, CLUSTER_RISCO, QUARTIL_COBERTURA, CLUSTER_FINAL);

CASE WHEN &PRODUTO = 'LEVES' THEN(

CASE WHEN &FL_BASE = 'ATIVO' THEN(

 CASE WHEN (&QTATRASO <=180) THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 3 OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN(2,3))) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 2)) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END)

	  WHEN (&QTATRASO >180 AND &QTATRASO <=270) THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 3)) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3))) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END)

	  WHEN (&QTATRASO >270 AND &QTATRASO <= 360) THEN (CASE WHEN ((&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') OR
                                                          (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 0 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO= 'REGULAR')) THEN 4

                                                     WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 3)) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3))) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END) END)

	  ELSE(CASE WHEN ((&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 0) OR
                     (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 1 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM')) THEN 4

                WHEN  (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2) OR
                      (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 1 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM')) THEN 3

				WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA =3) THEN 2 ELSE 1 END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO LIKE 'CVG%' THEN(

CASE WHEN &FL_BASE = 'ATIVO' THEN(

 CASE WHEN (&QTATRASO <=180) THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 3 OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN(2))) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 2)) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END)

	  WHEN (&QTATRASO >180 AND &QTATRASO <=270) THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 3)) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3))) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END)

	  WHEN &QTATRASO >270  THEN (CASE WHEN ((&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') OR
                                                          (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 0 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO= 'REGULAR')) THEN 4

                                                     WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 3)) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3))) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END) END)

	  ELSE(CASE WHEN ((&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 0) OR
                     (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 1 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM')) THEN 4

                WHEN  (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2) OR
                      (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 1 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM')) THEN 3

				WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA =3) THEN 2 ELSE 1 END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO = 'MOTOS' THEN(

CASE WHEN &FL_BASE = 'ATIVO' THEN(

 CASE WHEN (&QTATRASO <=180) THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 3 OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN(2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN(3,4))) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA =0) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 2)) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END)

	  WHEN (&QTATRASO >180 AND &QTATRASO <=270) THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 3 OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN(2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN(3,4))) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA =0) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 2)) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END)

	  WHEN &QTATRASO >270  THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (1,2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (4,5))) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3))) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END) END)

	  ELSE(CASE WHEN ((&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 0) OR
                     (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 1 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM')) THEN 4

                WHEN  (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2) OR
                      (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 1 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM')) THEN 3

				WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA =3) THEN 2 ELSE 1 END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO = 'PESADOS' THEN(

CASE WHEN &FL_BASE = 'ATIVO' THEN(

 CASE WHEN (&QTATRASO <=180) THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA =2) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (3,4))) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 2)) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END)

	  WHEN (&QTATRASO >180 AND &QTATRASO <=270) THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1,2)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA =2) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (3,4))) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 2)) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END)

	  WHEN &QTATRASO >270  THEN (CASE WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1) AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM') THEN 4

                                                     WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1,2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2,3)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (3,4))) THEN 2

													 WHEN ((&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'REGULAR' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (0,1)) OR
                                                          (&CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM' AND &QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 2)) THEN 3 ELSE 1 END) END)

	  ELSE(CASE WHEN ((&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 0) OR
                     (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 1 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'BOM')) THEN 4

                WHEN  (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA IN (2) OR
                      (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA = 1 AND &CLUSTER_RISCO = 'RUIM')) THEN 3

				WHEN (&QUARTIL_COBERTURA =3) THEN 2 ELSE 1 END) END) END AS &CLUSTER_FINAL

%MEND APLICA_CLUSTER;

%MACRO POLITICA_ATIVA_ESTRUTURANTE (PRODUTO, ATRASO, CLUSTER, COLLECTION, LETRA);

CASE WHEN &PRODUTO = 'LEVES' THEN(

 CASE WHEN  &ATRASO <=90 THEN 'O'

       WHEN (&ATRASO >91 AND &ATRASO <=120) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'J' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'L'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'N' ELSE 'O' END) END)

      WHEN (&ATRASO >120 AND &ATRASO <=180) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'L'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'N' ELSE 'O' END) END)

	   WHEN (&ATRASO >180 AND &ATRASO <=270) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'F'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'GW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'G' ELSE 'J' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                  WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END) END)

       WHEN &ATRASO >270 THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'FW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'M' ELSE 'GW' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'M'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'I' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END) END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO LIKE 'CVG%' THEN(

 CASE WHEN  &ATRASO <=90 THEN 'O'

       WHEN (&ATRASO >91 AND &ATRASO <=120) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'J' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'L'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'N' ELSE 'O' END) END)

      WHEN (&ATRASO >120 AND &ATRASO <=180) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'L'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'N' ELSE 'O' END) END)

	   WHEN (&ATRASO >180 AND &ATRASO <=270) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'F'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'GW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'G' ELSE 'J' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                  WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END) END)

       WHEN &ATRASO >270 THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'FW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'M' ELSE 'GW' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'M'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'I' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END) END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO = 'MOTOS' THEN(

 CASE WHEN  &ATRASO <=90 THEN 'O'

       WHEN (&ATRASO >91 AND &ATRASO <=120) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'J' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'L'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'N' ELSE 'O' END) END)

      WHEN (&ATRASO >120 AND &ATRASO <=180) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'L'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'N' ELSE 'O' END) END)

	   WHEN (&ATRASO >180 AND &ATRASO <=270) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'GW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'G' ELSE 'I' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'H'
                                                                  WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'L' ELSE 'N' END) END)

       WHEN &ATRASO >270 THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'M'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'GW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'G' ELSE 'I' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END) END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO = 'PESADOS' THEN(

 CASE WHEN  &ATRASO <=90 THEN 'O'

       WHEN (&ATRASO >91 AND &ATRASO <=120) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'J' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'L'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'N' ELSE 'O' END) END)

      WHEN (&ATRASO >120 AND &ATRASO <=180) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'GW'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'G' ELSE 'I' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'I'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'J' ELSE 'H' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'J'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'H' ELSE 'L' END) END)

	   WHEN (&ATRASO >180 AND &ATRASO <=270) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'FW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'F'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'GW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'G' ELSE 'I' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'G'
                                                                  WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'I' ELSE 'J' END) END)

       WHEN &ATRASO >270 THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'FW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'F'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'GW'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'G' ELSE 'I' END) END) END) END AS &LETRA

%MEND POLITICA_ATIVA_ESTRUTURANTE;

%MACRO POLITICA_WO_ESTRUTURANTE (PRODUTO, ATRASO, CLUSTER, COLLECTION, LETRA);

CASE WHEN &PRODUTO = 'LEVES' THEN(

CASE WHEN (&ATRASO <=450) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'E' ELSE 'FW' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'F'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END) END)

	   WHEN (&ATRASO >450 AND &ATRASO <=540) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'D' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'E' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'FW'
                                                                  WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >540 AND &ATRASO <=630) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
						                                                            WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'D' END)
						                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
					                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
					                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'FW' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
					                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'M' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >630 AND &ATRASO <=720) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'CW'
					                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'DW' END)
						                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
					                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
					                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'E' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
					                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >720 AND &ATRASO <=1800) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
						                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'CW' ELSE 'CW' END)
							                             WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'CW'
						                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'EW' END)
														 WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
						                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
														 ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
						                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >1800) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
				                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'C' ELSE 'C' END)
					                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
				                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'CW' ELSE 'DW' END)
												    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
				                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
												    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
				                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'FW' END) END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO LIKE 'CVG%' THEN(

CASE WHEN (&ATRASO <=450) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'E' ELSE 'FW' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'F'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END) END)

	   WHEN (&ATRASO >450 AND &ATRASO <=540) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'D' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'E' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'FW'
                                                                  WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >540 AND &ATRASO <=630) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'D' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'FW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'M' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >630 AND &ATRASO <=720) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'CW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'DW' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'E' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >720 AND &ATRASO <=1800) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'CW' ELSE 'CW' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'CW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >1800) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'C' ELSE 'C' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'CW' ELSE 'DW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'FW' END) END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO = 'MOTOS' THEN(

CASE WHEN (&ATRASO <=450) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'E' ELSE 'FW' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'F'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END) END)

	   WHEN (&ATRASO >450 AND &ATRASO <=540) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'D' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'E' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'FW'
                                                                  WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >540 AND &ATRASO <=630) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'D' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'FW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'M' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >630 AND &ATRASO <=720) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'CW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'DW' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'E' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >720 AND &ATRASO <=1800) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'CW' ELSE 'CW' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'CW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >1800) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'C' ELSE 'C' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'CW' ELSE 'DW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'FW' END) END) END)

WHEN &PRODUTO = 'PESADOS' THEN(

CASE WHEN (&ATRASO <=450) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'E' ELSE 'FW' END)
													   WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                                   WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END)
													   ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'F'
                                                                 WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'GW' ELSE 'G' END) END)

	   WHEN (&ATRASO >450 AND &ATRASO <=540) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'D' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'E' END)
													    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                                                                    WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END)
													    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'FW'
                                                                  WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'F' ELSE 'GW' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >540 AND &ATRASO <=630) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'D' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'FW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'E'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'M' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >630 AND &ATRASO <=720) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'CW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'DW' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'E' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >720 AND &ATRASO <=1800) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'CW' ELSE 'CW' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'CW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'DW' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'EW'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'FW' ELSE 'F' END) END)

       WHEN (&ATRASO >1800) THEN (CASE WHEN &CLUSTER = 4 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'C' ELSE 'C' END)
	                                WHEN &CLUSTER = 3 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'C'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'CW' ELSE 'DW' END)
								    WHEN &CLUSTER = 2 THEN(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'DW'
                                                                WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'D' ELSE 'EW' END)
								    ELSE(CASE WHEN &COLLECTION = 'RUIM' THEN 'D'
                                              WHEN &COLLECTION = 'REGULAR' THEN 'EW' ELSE 'FW' END) END) END) END AS &LETRA

%MEND POLITICA_WO_ESTRUTURANTE;
